- **quantile-normalization-of-count-matrices**: Use when tall-format filtered scRNA-seq count data must have per-cell expression distributions standardized across cells (e.g., to remove library-composition/distribution shifts) while preserving within-cell relative expression rank order, at scales requiring Spark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/quantile-normalization-of-count-matrices))
- **quantile-normalization-of-merged-expression-matrices**: Use when pseudo-bulk profiles computed from scRNA-seq (e.g. per pre-implantation stage from GSE38495/GSE45719) must be compared against bulk RNA-seq profiles from a different platform, where column-wise expression distributions differ and must be forced onto a common scale. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/quantile-normalization-of-merged-expression-matrices))
- **quantile-normalization-of-omics-matrices**: Use when raw metabolite-sample matrices (e.g., plasma_breast_cancer.csv) show sample-to-sample distribution shifts before pathway transformation or machine learning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/quantile-normalization-of-omics-matrices))
- **quantile-normalization-of-rna-seq-expression**: Use when a filtered RNA-seq expression matrix spanning multiple stress conditions must be made distribution-comparable across samples prior to computing gene-gene correlations for network construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/quantile-normalization-of-rna-seq-expression))
- **quantile-normalization-of-sample-distributions**: Use when an imputed metabolomics feature table (samples × metabolite features) shows sample-to-sample intensity distribution shifts to remove before statistical analysis, and simple per-sample scaling is insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/quantile-normalization-of-sample-distributions))
- **quantile-normalization-rna-seq**: Use when apply quantile normalization after filtering a Salmon-derived count matrix for low-abundance features using edgeR, and before batch-effect correction or differential expression analysis. - **quantile-normalized-microarray-data-processing**: Use when microarray intensity data has already been quantile-normalized via PIMENTo's Preprocess Data command and background noise must be separated from signal by choosing a log2 cutoff from the intensity histogram before downstream analysis.
